const clone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
function celebrationCode(blob){
  const s=String(blob||'');
  if(/annunciation|annuntiat|25[- /]03|03[- /]25/i.test(s)) return 'ANNUNCIATION';
  if(/christmas|nativity of (?:our )?lord|nativitas domini|25[- /]12|12[- /]25/i.test(s)) return 'CHRISTMAS';
  return 'OTHER';
}
function contextFromRuntime(extra={}){
  const st=globalThis.AO_RUNTIME_V8?.store?.getState?.()||{};
  const live=st.live||{};
  const active=globalThis.AO_ACTIVE_MASS_SESSION||{};
  const title=String(active?.proper?.title||active?.resolvedMass?.title||st?.resolution?.day?.main?.title||'');
  const proper=String(active?.resolvedMass?.properSource||active?.proper?.sourcePath||st?.resolution?.day?.selectedMassOption?.path||st?.resolution?.day?.main?.path||'');
  const settings=st.settings||{};
  const out={
    form:String(live.form||settings.massForm||'low').toUpperCase(),
    requiem:/requiem|defunct|dead|missa pro defunctis/i.test(title+' '+proper),
    celebration:title,
    celebrationCode:celebrationCode(title+' '+proper),
    properPath:proper,
    secondConfiteor:!!settings.secondConfiteor
  };
  return Object.assign(out,extra||{});
}
function postureFor(stepId,ctx={}){
  const c=contextFromRuntime(ctx);
  const group=DATA.step_to_posture_group[stepId];
  const p=group?DATA.posture_groups[group]:null;
  if(!p) return null;
  if(c.requiem&&group==='blessing') return null;
  const form=String(c.form||'LOW').toLowerCase();
  const action=p[form]||p.default||'FOLLOW_LOCAL_CUSTOM';
  return {group,action,authority:p.authority,localOverride:true};
}
function recordSuppressed(rec,c){
  if(rec.step==='second-confiteor'&&!c.secondConfiteor) return true;
  if(c.requiem&&rec.step==='agnus'&&rec.action==='BREAST_STRIKE_3') return true;
  if(c.requiem&&rec.step==='blessing') return true;
  return false;
}
function variantMatches(v,c){
  const w=v?.when||{};
  if(w.form&&String(c.form).toUpperCase()!==String(w.form).toUpperCase()) return false;
  if(Array.isArray(w.celebration_group)&&w.celebration_group.length&&!w.celebration_group.includes(c.celebrationCode)) return false;
  return true;
}
function resolvedRecord(rec,c){
  const out=clone(rec);
  for(const v of (out.conditional_variants||[])){
    if(!variantMatches(v,c)) continue;
    out.action=v.action||out.action;
    out.authority=v.authority||out.authority;
    out.sourceRefs=v.sourceRefs||out.sourceRefs;
    const label=DATA.authority_labels[out.authority];
    if(label&&out.guide){out.guide.status_en=label.en;out.guide.status_fr=label.fr;out.guide.sources=out.sourceRefs;}
    if(out.action==='KNEEL'&&out.step==='credo'){
      out.guide=Object.assign({},DATA.guide_library.KNEEL,{
        status_en:label?.en||'1962 rubric',
        status_fr:label?.fr||'Rubrique de 1962',
        sources:out.sourceRefs,
        action_en:'At a Sung Mass on Christmas or the Annunciation, kneel at Et incarnatus est and remain kneeling through et homo factus est.',
        action_fr:'À une Messe chantée de Noël ou de l’Annonciation, se mettre à genoux à Et incarnatus est et rester à genoux jusqu’à et homo factus est.',
        why_en:'This is the specific 1962 Incarnation exception; the ordinary genuflection becomes kneeling at these sung celebrations.',
        why_fr:'C’est l’exception propre de 1962 pour l’Incarnation ; la génuflexion ordinaire devient une posture à genoux lors de ces célébrations chantées.'
      });
    }
    break;
  }
  return out;
}
function gesturesFor(stepId,ctx={}){
  const c=contextFromRuntime(ctx);
  return DATA.fixed_gestures.filter(r=>r.step===stepId&&!recordSuppressed(r,c)).map(r=>resolvedRecord(r,c));
}
function guideFor(record,lang='en'){
  if(!record) return null;
  const g=record.guide||DATA.guide_library[record.action]||{};
  const sourceRefs=record.sourceRefs||g.sources||[];
  return {
    action:g[lang==='fr'?'action_fr':'action_en']||'',
    status:g[lang==='fr'?'status_fr':'status_en']||DATA.authority_labels[record.authority]?.[lang]||record.authority,
    why:g[lang==='fr'?'why_fr':'why_en']||'',
    sources:sourceRefs.map(id=>DATA.source_registry[id]).filter(Boolean)
  };
}
function resolveGuidance(stepId,ctx={}){
  const c=contextFromRuntime(ctx);
  const gestures=gesturesFor(stepId,c);
  return {
    step:stepId,
    context:c,
    posture:postureFor(stepId,c),
    gestures,
    guides:gestures.map(r=>guideFor(r,'en')),
    dynamicRules:DATA.dynamic_gestures,
    suppressions:DATA.suppressions
  };
}
function inspect(){
  const old=globalThis.AO_INLINE_CUES_V251;
  const checks=Object.assign({},DATA.audit.checks,{
    cueEngineAttached:!!old,
    cueEngineVersion:old?.version==='25.5',
    normalizedRegistryPass:DATA.audit.pass,
    gospelResolverSemantic:gesturesFor('gospel-announcement',{form:'LOW'}).some(x=>x.action==='GOSPEL_CROSSES_3'),
    noElevationResolverCross:gesturesFor('consecration-host',{form:'LOW'}).every(x=>x.action!=='SIGN_OF_CROSS'),
